# Commands · P · part 5

1,331 entries, plan-canvas to planejar. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **plan-canvas**: Produce a single-page planning canvas for a feature or change — intent, non-goals, users, constraints, invariants touched, risks, honest scope, test slice, and step-by-step.
